Wait, the key is in the red box. But Mia needs to make Ava think it's in the blue. So Mia should give clues that imply the blue box is correct. Maybe say, "The key is in the box that's not red." But that's direct. Wait, but that would be obvious. Alternatively, use a metaphor or a riddle. But the user said to be direct and concise. So maybe just point to the blue box, but not too directly.
